# Design authority and value lineage

Registry revision: 832 · Audit date: 2026-11-17 (America/Los_Angeles)


[H-577](../CHANGELOG.md#h-577)–[H-579](../CHANGELOG.md#h-579)/[K-GENERATOR-DRAFT-20261117](../CHANGELOG.md#k-generator-draft-20261117) introduces pending D-29r1/C-96/P-521–P-524. The human confirms enclosing-callable return from the loop body, proposes receiving-style headers and normal body feedback, and prefers void-only generator completion. The draft separates those directions, preserves D-17 lexical scopes/defer destinations, and identifies repeated reception, signature/owner proofs and nonlocal cleanup as pending. It selects no final data result for the whole for, escaping body, coroutine frame, new capture permission or implementation. Source417 and the separate byte-identical D-18r113 review remain unchanged.

[K-789](../CHANGELOG.md#k-789), source417, continues D-26/H-407/C-89/C-90/P-371/P-372/P-374/P-375, D-14/H-295 and existing D-18 complete-value coverage under H-420/H-423. One concrete scalar signature with invariant declarations can prove independent input domains against its fixed preparation and retain one checked implementation. Actual identities, selected interpretation, result/throws/body checks, complete captures and cleanup continue through the shared checked/native interface. Nonuniform obligations retain complete bounded enumeration and honest exhaustion diagnostics. Acceptance covers wide/mixed scalar views, inspection, recursive native reuse, replay, prepared callees and exact failure cleanup. Charter §§2/3.3/5.3/6.3/8/12 and D-16 remain; no new numeric/source/capture permission, permanent analysis/carrier/ABI or broader qualification follows. D-18r113 and receiving implementation reviews remain separate.

[H-576](../CHANGELOG.md#h-576)/[K-RECEIVE-OVERLAP-20261117](../CHANGELOG.md#k-receive-overlap-20261117) records D-22r33/D-23r23 and D-22-RECEIVE-OVERLAP/P-518–P-520: cross-entry receiving writes overlapping borrowed payload reads are author-responsibility undefined behavior. The human permits snapshotting/reordered reads and writes, plans warnings with opt-in error escalation, and allows expensive analysis to be deferred. This scoped source/diagnostic boundary does not make ordinary target sharing undefined, remove owner/access/init requirements or qualify the complete receiving/analysis/native mechanism. Other destination-write conflicts and exact grammar/scope remain open. Source416 and D-18r113 are unchanged.

[H-574](../CHANGELOG.md#h-574)/[H-575](../CHANGELOG.md#h-575)/[K-RECEIVING-SPEC-20261117](../CHANGELOG.md#k-receiving-spec-20261117) records D-22r33’s receiving specification: `{ val count <-; ref selected = node; existing <- field; } := producer();`. The human requires a declarative destination map without arbitrary expressions, not an executable receiving block. Declaration keywords and item operators express new/existing storage and initialization/rebinding/payload intent; omitted selectors use the simple lhs name. D-22-RECEIVE-SCOPE/P-515–P-517 retains pending exact grammar/scope, other destination-write conflicts and proof/value-path details; H-576 separately selects the borrowed-read UB boundary. D-23r23 preserves pre-RHS destination selection and its scalar matrix; new receivers are not inputs to other spec entries. Mainline source416/K-788 and the unchanged D-18r113 review remain independent.

[K-788](../CHANGELOG.md#k-788), source416, continues D-26/H-407/C-89/C-90/P-371/P-372/P-374/P-375, D-14/H-295 and existing D-18 family coverage under H-420/H-423. A detected input-coverage work limit reports its unchanged allowance and active callable view as resource exhaustion, leaving the obligation unproved. Acceptance covers ordinary/symbolic construction and declaration-only inspection, no partial output, below-budget native controls and unchanged supported-input LLVM/diagnostics. Charter §§2/3.3/5.3/6.3/8/12 and D-16 continue; no admission, enumeration, source exception, ABI or new qualification changes. H-572's proposal and D-18r113's separate review retain their recorded boundaries.

[H-572](../CHANGELOG.md#h-572)/[K-STRUCTURAL-RESULTS-20261117](../CHANGELOG.md#k-structural-results-20261117) records D-22r33’s preferred return-only structural result proposal, using `-> { ... }` without data: a labelled product of result contracts received as independent lexical variables, with no first-class group object, shared storage owner or structural sum. D-22-ANON-DATA/P-509–P-511 is pending for that new use restriction and detailed typing/transport; H-571’s named return, `:=` receiving, init and logical result-destination decisions remain confirmed. ## Design authority

[K-GENERATOR-DRAFT-20261117](../CHANGELOG.md#k-generator-draft-20261117), registry831→832, preserves D-GOV’s H-05/H-388/H-391/H-403/H-411 evidence discipline: register C-96/D-29 and candidate relationships without borrowing ancestor qualification. H-577’s explicit return target and H-578/H-579’s proposals/preferences retain their exact scopes; existing qualifications and source417 remain. Acceptance is traceable source provenance, current-only proposal content and unchanged dependency contracts, including D-16 and D-18r113.

[K-RETURNING-SYNC-20261116](../CHANGELOG.md#k-returning-sync-20261116) preserves K-761 compiler evidence alongside H-542–H-547 in source389/registry787. D-17r83/D-23r17 own explicit return recovery, controlled result inspection and scoped cancellation; locally recovered finally work preserves its suspended result, while escaping ordinary cancellation clears it. D-10r190/D-15r42/D-21r22 retain context, startup and native obligations. FIFO failures remain independent. D-17-RETURN-STATE specifies the bounded return-inspection profile and excludes owning capture; source support, complete value-path qualification and physical protocols remain separate; no compiler support or new human confirmation follows. Existing qualified owner/access/init contracts, H-525/H-537–H-541/H-550/H-551 and D-16 continue.

[K-EXCEPTION-IDENTITY-SYNC-20261116](../CHANGELOG.md#k-exception-identity-sync-20261116), source373/registry769, preserves K-752 compiler evidence and the H-510–H-522 exception checkpoint. D-17r83/D-15r42/D-10r190/D-21r22 retain their scoped decisions and open physical/observable boundaries. Same-identity catch-local tip selection requires stable exception identity and live backing; current five-word values, cause handles and single-pending-error execution do not establish the new construction/wrapper/reserve/queue contracts. Unaffected call/result/owner/initialization proofs and D-16 continue.

[K-773](../CHANGELOG.md#k-773) continues source401 under D-28-IDENTITY/C-95/P-391–P-392, D-14r116/C-65/P-258, D-17r83/C-63/P-235/P-236/C-72/P-280, D-18r107, confirmed D-23r17 scalar/ref/ptr rules and D-26r21/H-407. Shared conditions preserve nullness and observed immutable reference equality and inequality through calls, results and cleanup joins. Conditional writes and deferred reads use original incoming selections without treating borrowed placeholders as disjoint cells; finite formal-origin alternatives preserve necessary missing-write conditions at callers. One predicate-constraint path preserves unknown and impossible evidence for both outcomes. Known target bounds connect observed identities to concrete-cell conditions, including substitution of prepared actual arguments. Finite loop invariants require initialized selected targets at initial entry and every reaching backedge. Conditional selection summaries preserve sufficient invocation-entry initialization through deferred loops, copied/borrowed views and branch/handler joins; assumptions close before read validation. Prepared assignments retain sufficient facts about saved payloads and entry selections. Captured selection requirements cross nested cleanup without substitution through unrelated mutable slots and are translated at their owning proof frame. Borrowed-input read summaries project those conditions onto prepared actuals through direct, generic, recursive and callable-value invocation. Independent reverse-queue models and native failure injection cover repeated captures, later rebinding, delayed writes, missing initialization and exact release. Pending borrowed results retain sufficient saved-target initialization through widened loop targets, cleanup and replacement/alternative returns. The same formal-input projection and prepared-actual substitution serve read and result summaries. Independent return-order models include failed-registration prefixes; saved payload writes remain distinct from source-slot rebinding. Ref return-site validity, nullness, readonly access, owners, lifetime and declared failures remain separate obligations. Acceptance covers selected-cell writes and conditional borrowed reads, nested/direct/deferred loop models, conditional cleanup, complementary writes and consistent reference partitions across calls and prepared defers, independent alias partitions, returned/direct selections, generic/mutual recursion, saved/rebound/weak references, failed work and registration recovery, cleanup/loops, native sharing and exact release. Charter §§2/3.1–3.3/4.3/6.3/8/12, H-525, owner/access/init and D-16 continue. Identity alone supplies no write or stronger capability. Unknown/provisional origins and unresolved/null bounds cannot create a physical-target or write guarantee, and unproved terminal/exceptional outcomes retain their obligations. Wider caller-slot relationships and new roots/ABI/return-inspection/queue scopes remain separate. No new source rule, runtime representation or human confirmation follows.

[K-751](../CHANGELOG.md#k-751), source371, continues incoming-target presence under the confirmed owner/null/access/initialization constraints and the qualified ordinary completion, call/capture and finite native-coverage contracts. Proofs distinguish immutable target snapshots, payload initialization and mutable binding selections; declared checked outcomes remain obligations. D-14r116/D-17r83/D-18r107/D-26r21, D-16 and the separately scoped deferred-queue revision retain their boundaries. No source syntax, runtime representation or new value path is selected.

[K-DEFER-STACK-SYNC-20261116](../CHANGELOG.md#k-defer-stack-sync-20261116) integrates the H-503–H-509 design checkpoint with K-750 identity-guard coverage in source370. D-17r83/D-15r42/D-10r190/D-21r22 retain their scope and unresolved policies. Current source tests establish the implemented null/owner/call/cleanup invariants, not the revised deferred-queue behavior. The prior immediate-look-ahead report is superseded by the recorded list-position contract; no runtime policy or new value path is inferred by this merge.

[K-750](../CHANGELOG.md#k-750) continues source369 under D-28-IDENTITY/C-95/P-391–P-392 and the qualified stable null, owner/access and checked-NPE contracts. Acceptance: preserve stable IDs, value/dependency endpoints and kinds, current qualification, exclusions and unresolved decisions; retrieve original human evidence when exact scope matters. Maintenance grants no product qualification. Human decisions and agent assessments have distinct [changelog evidence locators](../CHANGELOG.md#authority-evidence-index).

## 1. Audit and reading rules

1. Start with the relevant **document scope**, its named relationships and directly needed contracts. Read the charter when changing a design; unchanged qualified scope permits the local-contract shortcut in AGENTS.
2. **Confirmed** covers the stated proposition/revision/scope. **Continued** is an agent's contract-preserving assessment from that human baseline. **Pending-human** and **needs-reconfirmation** do not authorize implementation; **superseded/rejected** scopes are historical. Direction, concept, whole document and implementation acceptance are distinct.
3. Use one entry per independently decidable contract scope. The linked product document owns detailed semantics, non-goals and acceptance; this registry owns qualification and evidence. A routine implementation checkpoint needs no new concept, value edge or repeated status paragraph. Record coherent maintenance in one brief continuity event.
4. Nodes name topics. `derives-from` explains value; `depends-on` identifies prerequisites; `constrains` bounds behavior. Read each edge's scope/status: an ID, dependency or ancestor approval grants no additional authority. Apply current scopes and supersession before historical revision wording. Candidate value paths stay candidate even when associated behavior is confirmed.
5. For impact review, find both endpoints in the relationship table and use their contract links; follow value descendants and dependencies separately. These links route review, not new delegation. Preserve unaffected qualified scope; escalate actual evidence gaps, contradictions or uncovered material choices.
6. H-386 requires topic-based human discussion and eventual whole-file approval of new designs. Ask for concrete decisions, not internal IDs; do not reopen settled preferences. Build/test/toolchain permissions remain independent.
7. Human history is append-only in Git: preserve the actual source, statement, time uncertainty, scope and exclusions. Corrections/withdrawals get new events. Read original evidence when exact scope matters; an index or agent summary cannot replace confirmation. The [changelog evidence index](../CHANGELOG.md#authority-evidence-index) provides targeted retrieval.

### Current supersession and audit boundaries

**Source compiler restructuring (H-399):** on `main`, historical implementation evidence in D-07/D-10/D-15/D-17/D-18 and the source fixtures describes the preserved `multi-dispatch` baseline, not current feature availability. The source foundation supports checked scalar methods, direct AOT specializations, shared general bodies, recursion, lexical scalar storage, nullable local views, scalar borrows with cross-call initialization and returned-target summaries, complete nested callable values with structural generic signatures, eager operator methods with checked builtin delegation, bounded intrinsic exceptions and prepared/deferred cleanup with recovery. Regional, abstract-input, general callable capture, richer relational proofs and symbolic import/general module-composition paths remain explicit implementation gaps. Source-preserving file selections retain isolated names/contexts, explicit publication and once-only encounter-ordered initialization. Ordinary import retains source definitions and signatures through receiver interpretations, shared aliases/extensions and inherited-overload conflict checks. Confirmed language contracts remain the target. [The source capability reference](../tests/cod/language/README.md) describes current supported/missing behavior.

These are scoped replacements, not blanket invalidations. Apply them to older wording; unaffected requirements remain. Detailed originals remain in H/K evidence.

| Scope | Current decision / evidence | Affected contracts |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Capture/function effects | H-180/H-181 preserves captured values/hidden owners and forbids dynamic-owner extraction; H-197/H-198 replaces readonly function snapshots with explicit-memory access. | D-10/D-13/D-14/D-18 |
| Composition/publication | H-212 assigns redefinition to mixin; H-218/H-220 requires explicit conformance and distinguishes adapter import from consumer visibility; H-230–H-233 separates identities/aliases and requires publication; H-245 forbids foreign-world editing. | D-07/D-10/D-12/D-15 |
| Static reference owners | H-417 requires exactly one statically provable owner for each ref/ptr; omitted annotations infer from initialization and non-unique/unknown owner evidence rejects. Owner proof is required at initialization, including for null views. It preserves runtime target choice within one owner and per-call symbolic owner instantiation. | D-14r87/D-18r91/D-23r9; D-10r147/D-24r7 dependencies; concrete-type evidence remains separate |
| Null owner syntax | H-418 requires an owner on the receiving declaration or null literal; bare ptr p:T = null rejects. H-419 withdraws outer syntax; here selects the current full lexical scope and a named region/generic owner uses existing owner proof. An enclosing variable carries its owner inward through =, including null views. Both explicit assignment/initialization sides must agree. H-440 uses is null; its bare null gets the comparison operand’s owner context, while explicit different-owner identity comparisons do not join reference owners. | D-14r99/D-10r150/D-23r13; P-282; no allocation, target read or lifetime extension |

Known gaps remain scoped above; shortening their records needs no new approval. Current contract revisions and qualification are maintained in the scope table.

**Type-associated declaration lookup:** H-483/D-15-TYPE-METHODS replaces H-226 only where a local definition previously extended a family implicitly selected by import of a type. Both type reuse forms now allow local shadowing. Explicit callable imports, source identities, H-232 existing explicit adaptation and H-253 conflict checks continue; a local shadow with an independent identity does not supply a source-helper implementation by spelling alone; explicit family selection retains its existing connection.

Node descriptions identify topics, not blanket approval. Relationship rows carry rationale/status; §2 carries current document qualification. Contract links support review in either direction through edge endpoints, without adding value or dependency edges. Inspect all edges naming a changed node, then both endpoints and their contracts; dependency cycles cannot prove value.

## 4. Relationship register

Each row preserves a distinct proposition, endpoints, relation kind, rationale and scope. Historical revisions identify evidence coverage; they do not override §2 or qualify later content. An unspecified formal value path remains pending-human. Local confirmations retain their stated extent; P-31 is rejected. Dependencies never grant authority.

### Founding relationships

| Edge | Child / relation | Parent / target | Rationale | Evidence / scope |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| E-01 | C-01 derives-from | V-01 | 用 GADT 保存业务意图，使意图不依附某环境的执行代码。 | H-01；方向性 confirmed。 |
| E-02 | C-02 derives-from | V-01 | 用不同原生 handlers 承接环境差异，保留业务程序。 | H-01；方向性 confirmed。 |
| E-03 | C-02 depends-on | C-01 | handler 解释的是所承载的业务意图；此依赖本身不授予任意 handler 方案。 | H-01；角色关系 confirmed。 |
| E-04 | C-01 derives-from | V-02 | 图同时表达领域数据、业务逻辑及 ontology，而非不透明代码。 | H-02；方向性 confirmed。 |
| E-05 | C-03 derives-from | V-02 | 多 domain regions 共存以实现数据和业务逻辑即时集成。 | H-02；方向性 confirmed。 |

## 5. Evidence access

[Human decisions and agent continuity](../CHANGELOG.md#authority-evidence-index) are indexed by stable H/K ID in the changelog. Retrieve the referenced immutable source for exact propositions, scope, provenance or exclusions. Current status and lineage remain authoritative in this registry.
